105	class Finding(NamedTuple):
106	    rule: str
107	    path: str
108	    line: int  # 0 when the finding has no single line
109	    message: str
110	
111	    def __str__(self) -> str:
112	        where = f"{self.path}:{self.line}" if self.line else self.path
113	        return f"{where}: [{self.rule}] {self.message}"
114	
115	
116	@dataclass
117	class Diff:
118	    added: dict[str, list[tuple[int, str]]] = field(default_factory=dict)
119	    removed: dict[str, list[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)
120	    deleted: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
121	
122	    def added_lines(self, path: str) -> set[int]:
123	        return {n for n, _ in self.added.get(path, [])}
124	
125	
126	# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- the diff
127	HUNK = re.compile(r"^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,\d+)? @@")
128	
129	
130	def _diff_path(raw: str) -> str:
131	    raw = raw.rstrip("\t")
132	    return raw[2:] if raw[:2] in {"a/", "b/"} else raw
133	
134	
135	def parse_diff(text: str) -> Diff:
136	    """Added lines (with new line numbers), removed lines and deleted files, per file."""
137	    diff = Diff()
138	    old = new = ""
139	    line_no = 0
140	    in_header = False
141	    for line in text.splitlines():
142	        if line.startswith("diff --git "):
143	            old, new, in_header = "", "", True
144	        elif in_header and line.startswith("--- "):
145	            old = _diff_path(line[4:])
146	        elif in_header and line.startswith("+++ "):
147	            new = _diff_path(line[4:])
148	            if new == "/dev/null":
149	                diff.deleted.add(old)
150	        elif match := HUNK.match(line):
151	            in_header, line_no = False, int(match[1])
152	        elif in_header:
153	            continue
154	        elif line.startswith("+"):
155	            diff.added.setdefault(new, []).append((line_no, line[1:]))
156	            line_no += 1
157	        elif line.startswith("-"):
158	            diff.removed.setdefault(old, []).append(line[1:])
159	    return diff
160	
161	
162	def add_untracked(diff: Diff, paths: list[str]) -> None:
163	    """An untracked file is all added lines."""
164	    for name in paths:
165	        path = Path(name)
166	        if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > 2_000_000:
167	            continue
168	        try:
169	            text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
170	        except (UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
171	            continue
172	        diff.added[name] = list(enumerate(text.splitlines(), start=1))
173	
174	
175	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- line checks
176	def is_outside(path: str) -> bool:
177	    return path.startswith(tuple(f"{folder}/" for folder in OUTSIDE))
178	
179	
180	def is_python(path: str) -> bool:
181	    if path.startswith("scripts/") or is_outside(path):
182	        return False
183	    return path.endswith((".py", ".pyi"))
184	
185	
186	def is_test_file(path: str) -> bool:
187	    parts = Path(path).parts
188	    name = parts[-1] if parts else ""
189	    return (
190	        "tests" in parts
191	        and (name.startswith("test_") or name.endswith("_test.py"))
192	        and not is_outside(path)
193	    )
194	
195	
196	def line_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
197	    found: list[Finding] = []
198	    for path, lines in diff.added.items():
199	        python, test = is_python(path), is_test_file(path)
200	        path_settings = not is_outside(path) and not path.endswith(DOC_SUFFIXES)
201	        for n, text in lines:
202	            if python and SUPPRESSION.search(text):
203	                found.append(Finding("suppression", path, n, "new suppression comment"))
204	            if path_settings and IMPORT_PATH_SETTING.search(text):
205	                found += [
206	                    Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, n, f"{folder}/ on an import path")
207	                    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items()
208	                    if word.search(text)
209	                ]
210	            if test and SKIP.search(text):
211	                found.append(Finding("test-skipped", path, n, "test skipped or marked xfail"))
212	            if Path(path).name not in LOCKFILES:
213	                found += [
214	                    Finding("secret", path, n, f"looks like a {kind} (value not shown)")
215	                    for kind, pattern in SECRETS.items()
216	                    if pattern.search(text)
217	                ]
218	    return found
219	
220	
221	def test_file_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
222	    found = [
223	        Finding("test-deleted", p, 0, "test file deleted") for p in diff.deleted if is_test_file(p)
224	    ]
225	    for path, lines in diff.removed.items():
226	        if not is_test_file(path) or path in diff.deleted:
227	            continue
228	        lost = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for t in lines)
229	        gained = sum(bool(ASSERTION.search(t)) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []))
230	        if lost > gained:
231	            found.append(
232	                Finding("assertion-removed", path, 0, f"{lost - gained} assertion line(s) removed")
233	            )
234	    return found
235	
236	
237	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- AST checks
238	def _touches(node: ast.AST, lines: set[int]) -> bool:
239	    start = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
240	    end = getattr(node, "end_lineno", start) or start
241	    return any(start <= n <= end for n in lines)
242	
243	
244	def _is_trivial(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> bool:
245	    """Only pass, `...` or `raise NotImplementedError`, after an optional docstring."""
246	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
247	    return all(_is_placeholder(s) for s in stmts)
248	
249	
250	def _task_stub(body: list[ast.stmt]) -> str | None:
251	    """The task an interface stub names: a body that is only `raise NotImplementedError("M1-T05")`."""
252	    stmts = body[1:] if body and _is_docstring(body[0]) else body
253	    if len(stmts) != 1 or not isinstance(stmts[0], ast.Raise):
254	        return None
255	    exc = stmts[0].exc
256	    if not (isinstance(exc, ast.Call) and _name(exc.func) == "NotImplementedError"):
257	        return None
258	    arg = exc.args[0] if len(exc.args) == 1 and not exc.keywords else None
259	    if (
260	        isinstance(arg, ast.Constant)
261	        and isinstance(arg.value, str)
262	        and TASK_ID.fullmatch(arg.value)
263	    ):
264	        return arg.value
265	    return None
266	
267	
268	def _is_docstring(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
269	    return (
270	        isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr)
271	        and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant)
272	        and isinstance(stmt.value.value, str)
273	    )
274	
275	
276	def _is_placeholder(stmt: ast.stmt) -> bool:
277	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Pass):
278	        return True
279	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Expr) and isinstance(stmt.value, ast.Constant):
280	        return stmt.value.value is Ellipsis
281	    if isinstance(stmt, ast.Raise) and stmt.exc is not None:
282	        exc = stmt.exc.func if isinstance(stmt.exc, ast.Call) else stmt.exc
283	        return _name(exc) == "NotImplementedError"
284	    return False
285	
286	
287	def _name(node: ast.AST) -> str | None:
288	    if isinstance(node, ast.Name):
289	        return node.id
290	    if isinstance(node, ast.Attribute):
291	        return node.attr
292	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
293	        return _name(node.func)
294	    return None
295	
296	
297	def _exempt_functions(tree: ast.Module) -> set[ast.AST]:
298	    """Abstract methods, overloads and Protocol members may have placeholder bodies."""
299	    exempt: set[ast.AST] = set()
300	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
301	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
302	            if {_name(d) for d in node.decorator_list} & EXEMPT_DECORATORS:
303	                exempt.add(node)
304	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef) and "Protocol" in {_name(b) for b in node.bases}:
305	            exempt.update(
306	                n for n in node.body if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))
307	            )
308	    return exempt
309	
310	
311	def _in_folder(name: str | None, folder: str) -> bool:
312	    return name is not None and (name == folder or name.startswith(f"{folder}."))
313	
314	
315	def _mentions(node: ast.AST, word: re.Pattern[str]) -> bool:
316	    return any(
317	        isinstance(n, ast.Constant) and isinstance(n.value, str) and word.search(n.value)
318	        for n in ast.walk(node)
319	    )
320	
321	
322	def _is_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
323	    return (
324	        isinstance(node, ast.Attribute)
325	        and node.attr == "path"
326	        and isinstance(node.value, ast.Name)
327	        and node.value.id == "sys"
328	    )
329	
330	
331	def _imported_modules(node: ast.AST) -> list[str | None]:
332	    """Absolute module names an import statement or an import_module call brings in."""
333	    if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
334	        return [alias.name for alias in node.names]
335	    if isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
336	        return [node.module] if node.level == 0 else []
337	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and _name(node.func) in {"import_module", "__import__"}:
338	        first = node.args[0] if node.args else None
339	        if isinstance(first, ast.Constant) and isinstance(first.value, str):
340	            return [first.value]
341	    return []
342	
343	
344	def _changes_sys_path(node: ast.AST) -> bool:
345	    """sys.path.insert/append/extend, site.addsitedir, or an assignment to sys.path."""
346	    if isinstance(node, ast.Call) and isinstance(node.func, ast.Attribute):
347	        func = node.func
348	        return func.attr == "addsitedir" or (
349	            func.attr in SYS_PATH_CALLS and _is_sys_path(func.value)
350	        )
351	    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign):
352	        return any(_is_sys_path(target) for target in node.targets)
353	    if isinstance(node, ast.AugAssign):
354	        return _is_sys_path(node.target)
355	    return False
356	
357	
358	def _outside_finding(path: str, node: ast.AST) -> Finding | None:
359	    """CR-08: an import from references/ or probes/, or either folder added to sys.path."""
360	    line = getattr(node, "lineno", 0)
361	    for folder, word in OUTSIDE.items():
362	        if any(_in_folder(name, folder) for name in _imported_modules(node)):
363	            return Finding(f"{folder}-import", path, line, f"import from {folder}/")
364	        if _changes_sys_path(node) and _mentions(node, word):
365	            return Finding(f"{folder}-path", path, line, f"{folder}/ added to sys.path")
366	    return None
367	
368	
369	def _asserts(func: ast.AST) -> bool:
370	    for node in ast.walk(func):
371	        if isinstance(node, ast.Assert):
372	            return True
373	        name = _name(node) if isinstance(node, ast.Call) else None
374	        if name and (
375	            name.startswith("assert") or name in {"raises", "warns", "deprecated_call", "fail"}
376	        ):
377	            return True
378	    return False
379	
380	
381	def ast_findings(path: str, lines: set[int]) -> list[Finding]:
382	    try:
383	        tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
384	    except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
385	        return []  # ruff reports syntax errors; a deleted file has nothing to check
386	    exempt, test = _exempt_functions(tree), is_test_file(path)
387	    found: list[Finding] = []
388	    for node in ast.walk(tree):
389	        if not _touches(node, lines):
390	            continue
391	        if outside := _outside_finding(path, node):
392	            found.append(outside)
393	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
394	            if (
395	                node not in exempt
396	                and not path.endswith(".pyi")
397	                and _is_trivial(node.body)
398	                and not _task_stub(node.body)
399	            ):
400	                found.append(
401	                    Finding("stub", path, node.lineno, f"`{node.name}` has a placeholder body")
402	                )
403	            if test and node.name.startswith("test") and not _asserts(node):
404	                found.append(
405	                    Finding(
406	                        "test-without-assertion",
407	                        path,
408	                        node.lineno,
409	                        f"`{node.name}` asserts nothing",
410	                    )
411	                )
412	        elif isinstance(node, ast.ExceptHandler) and _is_trivial(node.body):
413	            if (None if node.type is None else _name(node.type)) in TRIVIAL_EXCEPT:
414	                found.append(
415	                    Finding("swallowed-exception", path, node.lineno, "exception swallowed")
416	                )
417	    return found
418	
419	
420	def open_tasks() -> set[str]:
421	    """Task IDs with an unchecked line in tasks/todo.md (none when there is no todo)."""
422	    path = Path("tasks/todo.md")
423	    return set(OPEN_TASK.findall(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))) if path.is_file() else set()
424	
425	
426	def task_stub_findings(paths: list[str], at_exit: bool) -> list[Finding]:
427	    """Interface stubs anywhere in the project's code: each must name an open task, and at the
428	    milestone exit none may be left. Not diff-scoped: the stub was written by an earlier task."""
429	    open_ids, found = open_tasks(), []
430	    for path in paths:
431	        if not is_python(path) or is_test_file(path) or not Path(path).is_file():
432	            continue
433	        try:
434	            tree = ast.parse(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
435	        except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
436	            continue
437	        for node in ast.walk(tree):
438	            if not isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
439	                continue
440	            if not (task := _task_stub(node.body)):
441	                continue
442	            if at_exit:
443	                found.append(
444	                    Finding(
445	                        "stub-left",
446	                        path,
447	                        node.lineno,
448	                        f"`{node.name}` is still the stub for {task}",
449	                    )
450	                )
451	            elif task not in open_ids:
452	                found.append(
453	                    Finding(
454	                        "stub-task",
455	                        path,
456	                        node.lineno,
457	                        f"`{node.name}` is a stub naming {task}, which is not open in tasks/todo.md",
458	                    )
459	                )
460	    return found
461	
462	
463	# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the bar: tool config
464	MAX_KEYS = (
465	    "tool.ruff.lint.mccabe.max-complexity",
466	    "tool.size_limits.max_module_lines",
467	    "tool.size_limits.max_function_lines",
468	    "tool.vulture.min_confidence",
469	    "tool.pylint.similarities.min-similarity-lines",
470	)
471	MIN_KEYS = ("tool.coverage.report.fail_under", "tool.size_limits.min_module_lines")
472	TRUE_KEYS = ("tool.mypy.strict", "tool.pytest.ini_options.xfail_strict")
473	OFF_KEYS = ("tool.pylint.main.exit-zero",)  # turning one on silences a check
474	NO_SHRINK = ("tool.ruff.lint.select", "tool.ruff.lint.extend-select")
475	NO_GROW = (
476	    "tool.ruff.lint.ignore", "tool.ruff.lint.extend-ignore", "tool.ruff.exclude",
477	    "tool.ruff.extend-exclude", "tool.ruff.lint.exclude", "tool.deptry.exclude",
478	    "tool.deptry.extend_exclude", "tool.deptry.ignore", "tool.vulture.ignore_names",
479	    "tool.vulture.exclude", "tool.mypy.exclude", "tool.pylint.main.ignore-paths",
480	    "tool.pylint.main.ignore", "tool.pylint.main.ignore-patterns",
481	)  # fmt: skip
482	# Excluding the references or probes folder is not a loosening: neither is project code (CR-08).
483	OUTSIDE_EXCLUDE = re.compile(r"\^?(references|probes)/?\*{0,2}")
484	NO_GROW_TABLES = ("tool.ruff.lint.per-file-ignores", "tool.deptry.per_rule_ignores")
485	MUST_MATCH = ("tool.importlinter", "tool.mypy.overrides", "tool.pyright")
486	STRICT_FLAGS = ("--strict-markers", "--strict-config")
487	
488	
489	def _get(data: dict[str, Any], dotted: str) -> Any:
490	    for key in dotted.split("."):
491	        if not isinstance(data, dict) or key not in data:
492	            return None
493	        data = data[key]
494	    return data
495	
496	
497	def _as_set(value: Any) -> set[str]:
498	    if isinstance(value, str):
499	        return {value}
500	    return {str(v) for v in value} if isinstance(value, list) else set()
501	
502	
503	def _limit_findings(old: dict[str, Any], new: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
504	    pylint = _get(old, "tool.ruff.lint.pylint") or {}
505	    maxes = [*MAX_KEYS, *(f"tool.ruff.lint.pylint.{k}" for k in pylint)]
506	    out = [
507	        f"{k}: {_get(old, k)} -> {_get(new, k)}"
508	        for k in maxes
509	        if _loosened(_get(old, k), _get(new, k), +1)
510	    ]
511	    out += [
512	        f"{k}: {_get(old, k)} -> {_get(new, k)}"
513	        for k in MIN_KEYS
514	        if _loosened(_get(old, k), _get(new, k), -1)
515	    ]
516	    out += [
517	        f"{k} turned off" for k in TRUE_KEYS if _get(old, k) is True and _get(new, k) is not True
518	    ]
519	    out += [f"{k} turned on" for k in OFF_KEYS if _get(new, k) is True and _get(old, k) is not True]
520	    return out
521	
522	
523	def _loosened(was: Any, now: Any, direction: int) -> bool:
524	    if not isinstance(was, (int, float)):
525	        return False
526	    if not isinstance(now, (int, float)):
527	        return True  # a limit that disappeared
528	    return (now - was) * direction > 0
529	
530	
531	def _list_findings(old: dict[str, Any], new: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
532	    out = [f"{k} lost {sorted(_as_set(_get(old, k)) - _as_set(_get(new, k)))}" for k in NO_SHRINK]
533	    for k in NO_GROW:
534	        gained = _as_set(_get(new, k)) - _as_set(_get(old, k))
535	        out.append(f"{k} gained {sorted(g for g in gained if not OUTSIDE_EXCLUDE.fullmatch(g))}")
536	    for table in NO_GROW_TABLES:
537	        was, now = _get(old, table) or {}, _get(new, table) or {}
538	        out += [
539	            f"{table}.{key} gained {sorted(_as_set(codes) - _as_set(was.get(key)))}"
540	            for key, codes in now.items()
541	            if _as_set(codes) - _as_set(was.get(key))
542	        ]
543	    flags = _as_set((_get(new, "tool.pytest.ini_options.addopts") or "").split())
544	    out += [
545	        f"pytest addopts dropped {f}"
546	        for f in STRICT_FLAGS
547	        if f in str(_get(old, "tool.pytest.ini_options.addopts")) and f not in flags
548	    ]
549	    out += [
550	        f"{k} changed (boundaries and type config change only through build-setup)"
551	        for k in MUST_MATCH
552	        if _get(old, k) != _get(new, k)
553	    ]
554	    return [o for o in out if not o.endswith("[]")]
555	
556	
557	def config_findings(old_pyproject: Path, diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
558	    found = [
559	        Finding("bar-loosened", p, 0, "new tool config file overrides pyproject.toml")
560	        for p in diff.added
561	        if Path(p).name in CONFIG_FILES and not p.startswith("scripts/")
562	    ]
563	    old_text = old_pyproject.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if old_pyproject.is_file() else ""
564	    if not old_text or not Path("pyproject.toml").is_file():
565	        return found
566	    try:
567	        old, new = (
568	            tomllib.loads(old_text),
569	            tomllib.loads(Path("pyproject.toml").read_text(encoding="utf-8")),
570	        )
571	    except tomllib.TOMLDecodeError as exc:
572	        return [*found, Finding("bar-loosened", "pyproject.toml", 0, f"cannot parse: {exc}")]
573	    changes = _limit_findings(old, new) + _list_findings(old, new)
574	    return found + [Finding("bar-loosened", "pyproject.toml", 0, c) for c in changes]
575	
576	
577	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- CONSTRAINTS.md
578	RULE_REF = re.compile(r"^\s*(?:-\s*\*\*(CR-\d+)\*\*|\|\s*(CR-\d+)\s*\|)")
579	
580	
581	def _section(lines: list[str], title: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
582	    """1-based line range of a `## title` section, or (0, -1)."""
583	    start = next((i for i, t in enumerate(lines, 1) if t.strip().lower() == f"## {title}"), 0)
584	    if not start:
585	        return 0, -1
586	    end = next(
587	        (i - 1 for i, t in enumerate(lines, 1) if i > start and t.startswith("## ")), len(lines)
588	    )
589	    return start, end
590	
591	
592	def _table_rows(lines: list[str], start: int, end: int) -> list[tuple[int, list[str]]]:
593	    rows = []
594	    for n in range(start + 1, end + 1):
595	        text = lines[n - 1].strip()
596	        if not text.startswith("|"):
597	            continue
598	        cells = [c.strip() for c in text.strip("|").split("|")]
599	        if cells and cells[0] not in {"", "ID"} and not set(cells[0]) <= set("-: "):
600	            rows.append((n, cells))
601	    return rows
602	
603	
604	def constraints_findings(diff: Diff) -> list[Finding]:
605	    path = "CONSTRAINTS.md"
606	    if path not in diff.added and path not in diff.removed:
607	        return []
608	    still = {
609	        m.group(1) or m.group(2) for _, t in diff.added.get(path, []) if (m := RULE_REF.match(t))
610	    }
611	    gone = {
612	        m.group(1) or m.group(2) for t in diff.removed.get(path, []) if (m := RULE_REF.match(t))
613	    }
614	    found = [Finding("rule-removed", path, 0, f"{rule} removed") for rule in sorted(gone - still)]
615	    lines = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if Path(path).is_file() else []
616	    start, end = _section(lines, "exceptions")
617	    new_rows = diff.added_lines(path)
618	    found += [
619	        Finding("new-exception", path, n, f"new exception {cells[0]} needs the user's yes")
620	        for n, cells in _table_rows(lines, start, end)
621	        if n in new_rows
622	    ]
623	    return found
624	
625	
626	def load_exceptions(today: date) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
627	    """(rule cell, path glob) for each unexpired row in CONSTRAINTS.md's Exceptions table."""
628	    path = Path("CONSTRAINTS.md")
629	    if not path.is_file():
630	        return []
631	    lines = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
632	    allowed = []
633	    for _, cells in _table_rows(lines, *_section(lines, "exceptions")):
634	        if len(cells) < 6:
635	            continue
636	        expires = re.match(r"\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}", cells[5])
637	        if expires and date.fromisoformat(expires[0]) < today:
638	            continue
639	        allowed.append((cells[1], cells[2].strip("`")))
640	    return allowed
641	
642	
643	def is_excepted(finding: Finding, allowed: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> bool:
644	    rule_id = RULE_IDS.get(finding.rule)
645	    return bool(rule_id) and any(
646	        rule_id in rule and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding.path, glob) for rule, glob in allowed
647	    )
648	
649	
650	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- main
651	def run(
652	    diff: Diff, old_pyproject: Path, python_files: list[str] | None = None, at_exit: bool = False
653	) -> list[Finding]:
654	    found = line_findings(diff) + test_file_findings(diff)
655	    found += task_stub_findings(python_files or [], at_exit)
656	    for path in diff.added:
657	        if is_python(path):
658	            found += ast_findings(path, diff.added_lines(path))
659	    found += config_findings(old_pyproject, diff) + constraints_findings(diff)
660	    allowed = load_exceptions(datetime.now(tz=UTC).date())
661	    return [f for f in found if not is_excepted(f, allowed)]
662	
663	
664	def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
665	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
666	    parser.add_argument("--diff", type=Path, required=True)
667	    parser.add_argument("--untracked", type=Path, required=True)
668	    parser.add_argument("--old-pyproject", type=Path, required=True)
669	    parser.add_argument(
670	        "--python", type=Path, help="every Python file in the project, one per line"
671	    )
672	    parser.add_argument(
673	        "--exit", action="store_true", help="the milestone exit: no stub may be left"
674	    )
675	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
676	    diff = parse_diff(args.diff.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"))
677	    add_untracked(diff, args.untracked.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines())
678	    python_files = args.python.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines() if args.python else []
679	    findings = run(diff, args.old_pyproject, python_files, args.exit)
680	    if not findings:
681	        print("floor_guard: clean", file=sys.stderr)
682	        return 0
683	    print("\n".join(str(f) for f in sorted(findings)))
684	    print(
685	        f"floor_guard: {len(findings)} floor violation(s). Fix the code; a real exception goes "
686	        "through build-setup (Revise) and the Exceptions table in CONSTRAINTS.md.",
687	        file=sys.stderr,
688	    )
689	    return 1
690	
691	
692	if __name__ == "__main__":
693	    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

88:- **LOG-03** For a world and variant with at least 3 finished runs, a learning-curve chart shows evaluation score against environment steps: the mean across runs as a line, the lowest-to-highest range across runs as a band, and each baseline's mean score as a horizontal reference line.
90:- **LOG-04** Learning-curve charts follow the `dataviz` skill's guidance for form, colour and labelling.
92:- **LOG-05** Each world can add its own logged measures (e.g. snake length at death, hits per fish), which are charted the same way as the score.
94:- **LOG-06** The learner can view the latest logged values of a run while it trains.
135:- **EVAL-01** Every world and variant has a random-play baseline that picks each action uniformly from the allowed actions.
137:- **EVAL-02** Every world and variant has a rule-based baseline that the learner writes by hand, described in one sentence in the world's feature below.
139:- **EVAL-03** Final evaluation plays every final checkpoint and both baselines on the same 100 seeds, none of which was used in training or in-training evaluation.
141:- **EVAL-04** Whether the learned agent beats each baseline is tested with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill. It is given: the hypothesis "the trained agent scores better than the baseline"; H0 = the learned agent's mean score is no better than the baseline's, H1 = it is better (in the world's score direction); the statistic = difference in mean episode score, learned (averaged over the runs, per seed) minus baseline; the null built by randomly swapping the learned and baseline labels within each held-out seed (paired permutation, 10,000 permutations); α = 0.05 with Holm correction across all baseline comparisons in that world (2 per variant); the EVAL-03 held-out episodes. The learned agent passes only if both nulls are rejected.
143:- **EVAL-05** "Clearly beats" also needs every single run to beat the rule baseline: each run's final mean score is better than the rule-based baseline's mean score.
145:- **EVAL-06** Final evaluation produces one summary per world and variant: mean and range of learned scores across runs, both baseline means, the EVAL-04 test results and the EVAL-05 verdict.
160:- **CHECK-02** For each world and variant, 5 runs of a reference implementation of the same kind of method are trained on the same world, budget and seeds as the hand-written method, and evaluated as in EVAL-03.
190:- **SNAKE-01** The world is a 10 × 10 grid with one snake starting 3 cells long in the middle, and one food item on a random free cell.
192:- **SNAKE-02** Each step the snake chooses one of three actions: turn left, turn right or go straight.
194:- **SNAKE-03** Eating food grows the snake by one cell, adds 1 to the score and places new food on a random free cell.
196:- **SNAKE-04** The episode ends when the head hits a wall or the snake's body, or after 100 steps without eating.
198:- **SNAKE-05** The score is the food eaten in the episode (higher is better).
200:- **SNAKE-06** The snake observes a fixed list of numbers: danger straight ahead, left and right; its heading; the food's direction relative to its head; and its length.
202:- **SNAKE-07** The rule-based baseline moves toward the food along the shorter axis, unless that move would end the episode next step, in which case it takes any move that does not.
204:- **SNAKE-08** The trained snake beats both baselines as in EVAL-04 and EVAL-05.
219:- **FISH-02** Each step a fish chooses a push as two numbers between −1 and 1 (left-right and up-down); its speed changes smoothly with the push, up to a top speed below the ball's.
233:- **FISH-09** The rule-based baseline pushes each fish directly away from the ball at full strength (from the ball it sees, or randomly when it sees none).
235:- **FISH-10** Every variant's trained fish beat both baselines as in EVAL-04 and EVAL-05.
252:- **PREY-02** Prey and predators move by smooth pushes as in FISH-02; predators' top speed is 1.2 × the prey's.
264:- **PREY-08** Each trained team is evaluated against fixed opponents: trained prey against the rule-based predators, compared with random and rule-based prey; trained predators against the rule-based prey, compared with random and rule-based predators. Each passes as in EVAL-04 and EVAL-05.
286:- **QR-05** Final evaluation (EVAL-03 and EVAL-04) of one world and variant finishes within 10 minutes on the laptop.
304:| 2026-10-17 | Checkpoint count fixed to 11 (0%–100% every 10%), as its example listed; pairing grid follows | routed from test-planning | TRAIN-03, PREY-09 (changed) |

U03.S33Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-adb398ddea3959632/src/rllab/core/types.py (199 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-adb398ddea3959632/src/rllab/core/types.py
"""The shared types, protocols and held-out seed sets every component speaks (contract: core).

`MethodConfig` and `RunConfig` are defined here too, though `methods` and `train` export them:
`runlog` records both (`RunRecord.config`, `Checkpoint.methods`) and sits below those two
components, so the definitions live at the bottom layer and the owners re-export them.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol

import numpy as np
from numpy.typing import NDArray

Direction = Literal["higher", "lower"]
Settings = Mapping[str, int | float | str | bool]
Array = NDArray[Any]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Discrete:
    """A choice from `n` actions: ints in `[0, n)`."""

    n: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Box:
    """`dims` numbers, each in `[low, high]`."""

    dims: int
    low: float
    high: float


ActionSpace = Discrete | Box


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TeamSpec:
    """A named team: its agents' indices, its score's name and direction."""

    name: str
    agents: tuple[int, ...]
    score_name: str
    direction: Direction


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MeasureSpec:
    """A world's own logged measure (LOG-05), charted like the score."""

    name: str
    team: str
    direction: Direction


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WorldSpec:
    """Everything a world declares before any episode starts (WORLD-04)."""

    world: str
    variant: str
    agents: tuple[str, ...]
    teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]
    obs_len: int
    action: ActionSpace
    max_steps: int
    measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]
    sight: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Step:
    """One world step for all agents: float32 `obs` (n × obs_len), `rewards`, bool `active`."""

    obs: Array
    rewards: Array
    active: Array
    done: bool
    truncated: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EpisodeResult:
    """One episode's totals: scores and rewards per team, measures by name."""

    seed: int
    length: int
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    team_rewards: Mapping[str, float]
    measures: Mapping[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Drawable:
    """One object on screen; `agent` is set for agents."""

    kind: str
    x: float
    y: float
    size: float
    team: str | None
    agent: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DrawState:
    """Every object on screen at one step (WORLD-06)."""

    width: float
    height: float
    objects: tuple[Drawable, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Frame:
    """One traced step: what to draw, running team scores, latest per-agent rewards."""

    step: int
    draw: DrawState
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    rewards: Array


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Episode:
    """A played episode; `frames` only when traced, `result.length + 1` of them."""

    result: EpisodeResult
    frames: tuple[Frame, ...] | None
    hit_step_limit: bool


class ActionError(ValueError):
    """An action outside the world's `ActionSpace`; it is never clipped."""

    def __init__(self, agent: str, value: object) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"agent {agent!r}: action {value!r} is outside the action space")
        self.agent = agent
        self.value = value


class World(Protocol):
    """A seeded multi-agent world (AD-04)."""

    spec: WorldSpec

    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...

    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...

    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...

    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...


class Policy(Protocol):
    """Acts for the `k` agents of one team: `k × obs_len` observations to `k` actions."""

    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...


EVAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(1_000_000, 1_000_020))
FINAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(2_000_000, 2_000_100))


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Output:
    """A written chart, video or write-up, and the runs, checkpoints and seeds behind it."""

    path: Path
    kind: Literal["chart", "video", "writeup"]
    made_from: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MethodConfig:
    """A learning method and its full settings (exported by `methods`)."""

    method: Literal["dqn", "ppo"]
    settings: Settings


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunConfig:
    """One training run's settings (exported by `train`)."""

    name: str
    world: str
    variant: str
    methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]
    seed: int
    budget: int
    check_settings: Mapping[str, Settings]
    device: str = "cpu"

"""Baseline types (contract: baselines)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

from rllab.core.types import Policy


class RulePolicy(Policy, Protocol):
    """A world's hand-written rule acting on observations only; `description` is one sentence."""

    description: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BaselineMeans:
    """Random and rule mean scores per team over the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes."""

    world: str
    variant: str
    random: Mapping[str, float]
    rule: Mapping[str, float]

"""Learner types and errors (contract: methods). `MethodConfig` is defined in `core.types`."""

from collections.abc import Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

import numpy as np

from rllab.core.types import Array, MethodConfig, Policy

__all__ = ["Learner", "MethodConfig", "NonFiniteError", "SpecMismatchError", "Transition"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Transition:
    """One world step for one team, rows in `TeamSpec.agents` order; `active` masks leavers."""

    obs: Array
    actions: Array
    rewards: Array
    next_obs: Array
    done: bool
    truncated: bool
    active: Array


class NonFiniteError(ArithmeticError):
    """A loss or parameter became NaN or infinite; raised before any parameter changes."""

    def __init__(self, value: float) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"non-finite value {value!r}")
        self.value = value


class SpecMismatchError(ValueError):
    """A checkpoint's `WorldSpec` differs from the world's in the named fields."""

    def __init__(self, fields: Sequence[str]) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"checkpoint spec differs from the world's in: {', '.join(fields)}")
        self.fields = tuple(fields)


class Learner(Protocol):
    """A hand-written learner for one team (CHECK-01)."""

    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...

    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...

    def policy(self) -> Policy: ...

    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-adb398ddea3959632/src/rllab/runlog/types.py
"""Run folder types (contract: runlog)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal, Protocol

from rllab.core.types import EpisodeResult, MethodConfig, RunConfig, WorldSpec


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Failure:
    """Where a failed run stopped: the step, the invalid value and the last good checkpoint."""

    step: int
    value: float
    checkpoint: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunRecord:
    """A run's `run.json` (LOG-01): config with full settings, spec, times and status."""

    config: RunConfig
    spec: WorldSpec
    started: datetime
    ended: datetime | None
    status: Literal["running", "finished", "incomplete", "failed"]
    failure: Failure | None
    path: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class EvalPoint:
    """Mean scores per team and means per measure over the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` at one step."""

    step: int
    scores: Mapping[str, float]
    measures: Mapping[str, float]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Checkpoint:
    """One saved checkpoint: spec, method configs and per-team state at `pct` of the budget."""

    run: str
    pct: int
    step: int
    spec: WorldSpec
    methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]
    state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]
    path: Path


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Progress:
    """A live snapshot of a run, at most one evaluation point behind it (LOG-06)."""

    step: int
    budget: int
    latest: EvalPoint | None
    elapsed: timedelta
    status: str


class RunExistsError(FileExistsError):
    """The run folder already exists; nothing was written."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"run folder {path} already exists")
        self.path = path


class Run(Protocol):
    """A read-only view of one run folder, readable while the run writes."""

    record: RunRecord

    def episodes(self) -> list[EpisodeResult]: ...

    def evals(self) -> list[EvalPoint]: ...

    def checkpoints(self) -> list[Checkpoint]: ...

"""The run folder writer (AD-06)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.core.types import EpisodeResult, RunConfig, WorldSpec
from rllab.runlog.types import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, Failure, RunRecord


class RunWriter:
    """Writes `runs/<world>/<variant>/<name>/`; `RunExistsError` if that folder exists."""

    def __init__(self, root: Path, config: RunConfig, spec: WorldSpec) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def eval(self, point: EvalPoint) -> None:
        """Append `point` and flush every log."""
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def checkpoint(self, pct: int, step: int, state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]) -> Checkpoint:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

    def close(self, status: str, failure: Failure | None = None) -> RunRecord:
        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

"""Training types and errors (contract: train). `RunConfig` is defined in `core.types`."""

from dataclasses import dataclass

from rllab.core.types import RunConfig

__all__ = ["CheckFailedError", "CheckResult", "RunConfig"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CheckResult:
    """A method's check-world result: share of states where it picks the best action."""

    method: str
    world: str
    agreement: float
    passed: bool


class CheckFailedError(RuntimeError):
    """A method failed its check world, so no run of the batch starts."""

    def __init__(self, result: CheckResult) -> None:
        super().__init__(
            f"{result.method} failed its check on {result.world}: "
            f"agreement {result.agreement:.2f} is below 0.95"
        )
        self.result = result

"""Chart errors (contract: charts)."""

from collections.abc import Sequence


class MixedRunsError(ValueError):
    """The runs of one chart differ by more than their seed, in the named settings."""

    def __init__(self, settings: Sequence[str]) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"runs differ by more than seed, in: {', '.join(settings)}")
        self.settings = tuple(settings)

"""Final evaluation types (contract: evaluate)."""

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Comparison:
    """One learned-vs-baseline test; `difference` is positive when the learned agent is better."""

    baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]
    team: str
    learned_mean: float
    baseline_mean: float
    difference: float
    p_raw: float
    p_holm: float
    rejected: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Verdict:
    """The EVAL-05 verdict: both nulls rejected and every run beating the rule mean."""

    beats_baselines: bool
    failing_runs: tuple[str, ...]
    runs_needed: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Summary:
    """One world, variant and team's final evaluation (EVAL-06)."""

    world: str
    variant: str
    team: str
    runs: tuple[str, ...]
    learned_mean: float
    learned_range: tuple[float, float]
    run_means: Mapping[str, float]
    random_mean: float
    rule_mean: float
    comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]
    verdict: Verdict
    collapse: int | None

"""PL-06: Final evaluation.

The entry plays serially (`final_evaluation`'s default workers); it gains `workers` when a
caller first passes one (QR-05, M2; CR-22 allows 5 arguments).
"""

from collections.abc import Sequence
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.evaluate.types import Summary


def run_evaluate(
    root: Path, world: str, variant: str, out: Path, *, include: Sequence[str] = ()
) -> list[Summary]:
    """The final evaluation of the runs of `world` and `variant` under `root`."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T24")


def smoke() -> list[Summary]:
    """`rllab evaluate --smoke`: PL-06 end to end on small run folders."""
    raise NotImplementedError("M1-T24")

wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-adb398ddea3959632/src/rllab/cli/commands.py
"""The `rllab` command: parse arguments, call the pipeline entry, print its result (AD-10)."""

import argparse
import sys
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

from rllab.cli.pipelines import pl04_watch, pl05_chart, pl06_evaluate
from rllab.train.pipelines import pl01_check, pl03_batch

RUNS = Path("runs")


def parse_seeds(text: str) -> list[int]:
    """`1-5` or `1,3,5` (or both, `1-3,7`) to a list of seeds."""
    seeds: list[int] = []
    for part in text.split(","):
        first, _, last = part.partition("-")
        seeds.extend(range(int(first), int(last or first) + 1))
    return seeds


def _needed(value: Path | None, what: str) -> Path:
    if value is None:
        raise ValueError(f"{what} is needed (or --smoke)")
    return value


def _check(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    if args.smoke:
        return pl01_check.smoke()
    return pl01_check.run_check(_needed(args.config, "a config path"), args.seed)


def _train(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    if args.smoke:
        return pl03_batch.smoke()
    config = _needed(args.config, "a config path")
    return pl03_batch.run_batch(config, [args.seed], args.root)


def _batch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    if args.smoke:
        return pl03_batch.smoke()
    config = _needed(args.config, "a config path")
    return pl03_batch.run_batch(config, args.seeds, args.root, workers=args.workers)


def _watch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    if args.smoke:
        return pl04_watch.smoke()
    return pl04_watch.run_watch(_needed(args.run_path, "a run path"))


def _world(args: argparse.Namespace) -> str:
    if args.world is None:
        raise ValueError("a world is needed (or --smoke)")
    return str(args.world)


def _chart(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    if args.smoke:
        return pl05_chart.smoke()
    world = _world(args)
    out = args.out or Path("reports") / world
    return pl05_chart.run_chart(args.root, world, args.variant, out, include=args.include)


def _evaluate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
    if args.smoke:
        return pl06_evaluate.smoke()
    world = _world(args)
    today = datetime.now(UTC).date().isoformat()
    out = args.out or args.root / world / args.variant / "_eval" / today
    return pl06_evaluate.run_evaluate(args.root, world, args.variant, out, include=args.include)


def _command(
    subs: "argparse._SubParsersAction[argparse.ArgumentParser]",
    name: str,
    run: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], object],
    help_text: str,
) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    sub = subs.add_parser(name, help=help_text)
    sub.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="run end to end on built-in inputs")
    sub.set_defaults(run=run)
    return sub


def _config_command(
    subs: "argparse._SubParsersAction[argparse.ArgumentParser]",
    name: str,
    run: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], object],
    help_text: str,
) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    sub = _command(subs, name, run, help_text)
    sub.add_argument("config", type=Path, nargs="?", help="a TOML file in configs/")
    sub.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=RUNS, help="the runs folder")
    return sub


def _runs_command(
    subs: "argparse._SubParsersAction[argparse.ArgumentParser]",
    name: str,
    run: Callable[[argparse.Namespace], object],
    help_text: str,
) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    sub = _command(subs, name, run, help_text)
    sub.add_argument("world", nargs="?")
    sub.add_argument("variant", nargs="?", default="default")
    sub.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=RUNS, help="the runs folder")
    sub.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=None, help="where to write")
    sub.add_argument(
        "--include", action="append", default=[], help="also an unfinished run, by name"
    )
    return sub


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="rllab", description=__doc__)
    subs = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    check = _config_command(subs, "check", _check, "train each method on its check world")
    check.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    train = _config_command(subs, "train", _train, "one training run (a one-seed batch)")
    train.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    batch = _config_command(subs, "batch", _batch, "the method check, then a run per seed")
    batch.add_argument("--seeds", type=parse_seeds, default=[1], help="e.g. 1-5")
    batch.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
    watch = _command(subs, "watch", _watch, "the latest logged values of a run")
    watch.add_argument("run_path", type=Path, nargs="?")
    _runs_command(subs, "chart", _chart, "the learning-curve chart of a world's runs")
    _runs_command(subs, "evaluate", _evaluate, "the final evaluation of a world's runs")
    return parser


def _show(result: object) -> None:
    for item in result if isinstance(result, list) else [result]:
        print(item)


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Dispatch one subcommand to its pipeline entry and print what it returns."""
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = args.run(args)
    except Exception as exc:  # every failure is one line naming its cause, exit code 1
        print(f"rllab {args.command}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    _show(result)
    return 0

U03.S101Write/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-adb398ddea3959632/tests/contracts/contracts.py (357 lines)
wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-adb398ddea3959632/tests/contracts/contracts.py
"""Loading the frozen boundary contracts in tests/contracts/<component>.<Symbol>/ (CR-09).

Each folder holds, as its contract.md section asks:
- a type: `schema.json` (JSON Schema; `x-python` names the type) and `samples/<case>.json`
- a Protocol or a function another component calls: `double.py` replaying `recordings/<case>.json`
- a function another pipeline calls: `fake.py` serving its fixtures

`double.py` and `fake.py` define `build(fx: Fixtures, case: str) -> object`; they never import
this module, they get it through `fx`. From any test, load this module by path:

    import runpy
    contracts = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).parents[2] / "contracts" / "contracts.py"))
    spec = contracts["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "snake")
    world = contracts["double"]("core.World")
    make = contracts["fake"]("worlds.make")

JSON forms: a dataclass is an object of its fields (`Discrete` and `Box` add `"kind"`), an
array is `{"dtype", "shape", "data"}`, a tuple is a list, a `Path` or `datetime` a string, a
`timedelta` its seconds.
"""

import dataclasses
import datetime as dt
import importlib
import json
import types
import typing
from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import Box, Discrete

HERE = Path(__file__).parent
KINDS: dict[str, type] = {"discrete": Discrete, "box": Box}


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- JSON forms
def encode(value: Any) -> Any:
    """The JSON form of a contract value."""
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
        out = {f.name: encode(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
        kind = next((k for k, cls in KINDS.items() if isinstance(value, cls)), None)
        return {"kind": kind, **out} if kind else out
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {str(k): encode(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
        return [encode(v) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, dt.datetime):
        return value.isoformat()
    if isinstance(value, dt.timedelta):
        return value.total_seconds()
    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
        return value.item()
    return value


def _fail(where: str, why: str) -> typing.NoReturn:
    raise ValueError(f"{where or '<root>'}: {why}")


def _decode_dataclass(tp: type, data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        _fail(where, f"expected an object for {tp.__name__}")
    data = dict(data)
    kind = data.pop("kind", None)
    if kind is not None and KINDS.get(kind) is not tp:
        _fail(where, f"kind {kind!r} is not {tp.__name__}")
    hints = typing.get_type_hints(tp)
    fields = {f.name: f for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)}
    if extra := set(data) - set(fields):
        _fail(where, f"unknown fields {sorted(extra)} for {tp.__name__}")
    missing = [
        n
        for n, f in fields.items()
        if n not in data
        and f.default is dataclasses.MISSING
        and f.default_factory is dataclasses.MISSING
    ]
    if missing:
        _fail(where, f"missing fields {missing} for {tp.__name__}")
    return tp(**{n: decode(hints[n], v, f"{where}.{n}") for n, v in data.items()})


def _decode_union(args: tuple[Any, ...], data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
    if data is None and type(None) in args:
        return None
    if isinstance(data, dict) and "kind" in data:
        cls = KINDS.get(data["kind"])
        if cls in args:
            return _decode_dataclass(cls, data, where)
    errors = []
    for arg in args:
        if arg is type(None):
            continue
        try:
            return decode(arg, data, where)
        except ValueError as exc:
            errors.append(str(exc))
    _fail(where, f"matches no member of the union: {errors}")


def _decode_array(data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
    if not isinstance(data, dict) or set(data) != {"dtype", "shape", "data"}:
        _fail(where, "expected an array object {dtype, shape, data}")
    array = np.asarray(data["data"], dtype=np.dtype(data["dtype"]))
    return array.reshape(data["shape"])


def _decode_scalar(tp: type, data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
    if tp is bool:
        if not isinstance(data, bool):
            _fail(where, f"expected a bool, got {data!r}")
        return data
    if tp is int:
        if not isinstance(data, int) or isinstance(data, bool):
            _fail(where, f"expected an int, got {data!r}")
        return data
    if tp is float:
        if not isinstance(data, (int, float)) or isinstance(data, bool):
            _fail(where, f"expected a number, got {data!r}")
        return float(data)
    if tp is str:
        if not isinstance(data, str):
            _fail(where, f"expected a string, got {data!r}")
        return data
    if tp is Path:
        return Path(decode(str, data, where))
    if tp is dt.datetime:
        return dt.datetime.fromisoformat(decode(str, data, where))
    if tp is dt.timedelta:
        return dt.timedelta(seconds=decode(float, data, where))
    _fail(where, f"no JSON form for {tp!r}")


def _decode_container(origin: Any, args: tuple[Any, ...], data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
    if origin in (Mapping, dict):
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            _fail(where, "expected an object")
        key, value = args or (str, Any)
        return {decode(key, k, where): decode(value, v, f"{where}.{k}") for k, v in data.items()}
    if not isinstance(data, list):
        _fail(where, "expected a list")
    if origin is list:
        return [decode(args[0], v, f"{where}[{i}]") for i, v in enumerate(data)]
    if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
        return tuple(decode(args[0], v, f"{where}[{i}]") for i, v in enumerate(data))
    if len(args) != len(data):
        _fail(where, f"expected {len(args)} items, got {len(data)}")
    return tuple(decode(a, v, f"{where}[{i}]") for i, (a, v) in enumerate(zip(args, data)))


def decode(tp: Any, data: Any, where: str = "") -> Any:
    """`data` (a JSON form) as a value of type `tp`, checked strictly; `ValueError` if not."""
    origin, args = typing.get_origin(tp), typing.get_args(tp)
    if tp is Any or tp is object:
        return data
    if isinstance(tp, typing.TypeAliasType):
        return decode(tp.__value__, data, where)
    if origin is typing.Literal:
        if data not in args:
            _fail(where, f"{data!r} is not one of {args}")
        return data
    if origin in (typing.Union, types.UnionType):
        return _decode_union(args, data, where)
    if origin is np.ndarray or tp is np.ndarray:
        return _decode_array(data, where)
    if origin in (Mapping, dict, list, tuple):
        return _decode_container(origin, args, data, where)
    if isinstance(tp, type) and dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):
        return _decode_dataclass(tp, data, where)
    return _decode_scalar(tp, data, where)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------- JSON Schema (subset)
JSON_TYPES: dict[str, tuple[type, ...]] = {
    "object": (dict,),
    "array": (list,),
    "string": (str,),
    "integer": (int,),
    "number": (int, float),
    "boolean": (bool,),
    "null": (type(None),),
}


def _is_type(data: Any, name: str) -> bool:
    if isinstance(data, bool) and name in {"integer", "number"}:
        return False
    return isinstance(data, JSON_TYPES[name])


def _check_object(data: dict[str, Any], schema: dict[str, Any], root: dict[str, Any], where: str):
    for name in schema.get("required", []):
        if name not in data:
            _fail(where, f"missing required property {name!r}")
    props = schema.get("properties", {})
    extra = schema.get("additionalProperties", True)
    for key, value in data.items():
        if key in props:
            validate(value, props[key], root, f"{where}.{key}")
        elif extra is False:
            _fail(where, f"unexpected property {key!r}")
        elif isinstance(extra, dict):
            validate(value, extra, root, f"{where}.{key}")


def _check_array(data: list[Any], schema: dict[str, Any], root: dict[str, Any], where: str):
    if len(data) < schema.get("minItems", 0):
        _fail(where, f"fewer than {schema['minItems']} items")
    if "maxItems" in schema and len(data) > schema["maxItems"]:
        _fail(where, f"more than {schema['maxItems']} items")
    prefix = schema.get("prefixItems", [])
    for i, item in enumerate(data):
        sub = prefix[i] if i < len(prefix) else schema.get("items")
        if sub is False:
            _fail(where, f"unexpected item {i}")
        if isinstance(sub, dict):
            validate(item, sub, root, f"{where}[{i}]")


def _check_number(data: float, schema: dict[str, Any], where: str):
    if "minimum" in schema and data < schema["minimum"]:
        _fail(where, f"{data} is below the minimum {schema['minimum']}")
    if "maximum" in schema and data > schema["maximum"]:
        _fail(where, f"{data} is above the maximum {schema['maximum']}")


def _check_choice(data: Any, schema: dict[str, Any], root: dict[str, Any], where: str):
    for key in ("oneOf", "anyOf"):
        if key not in schema:
            continue
        passed = 0
        for sub in schema[key]:
            try:
                validate(data, sub, root, where)
                passed += 1
            except ValueError:
                continue
        if passed == 0 or (key == "oneOf" and passed > 1):
            _fail(where, f"{key}: {passed} of {len(schema[key])} alternatives match")


def validate(data: Any, schema: dict[str, Any], root: dict[str, Any] | None = None, where=""):
    """Check `data` against the JSON Schema subset these folders use; `ValueError` if not.

    Keywords: $ref (local), type, enum, const, minimum, maximum, properties, required,
    additionalProperties, items, prefixItems, minItems, maxItems, oneOf, anyOf."""
    root = schema if root is None else root
    if "$ref" in schema:
        target: Any = root
        for part in schema["$ref"].removeprefix("#/").split("/"):
            target = target[part]
        validate(data, target, root, where)
    if "type" in schema:
        names = schema["type"] if isinstance(schema["type"], list) else [schema["type"]]
        if not any(_is_type(data, n) for n in names):
            _fail(where, f"{data!r} is not of type {names}")
    if "enum" in schema and data not in schema["enum"]:
        _fail(where, f"{data!r} is not one of {schema['enum']}")
    if "const" in schema and data != schema["const"]:
        _fail(where, f"{data!r} is not {schema['const']!r}")
    if isinstance(data, (int, float)) and not isinstance(data, bool):
        _check_number(data, schema, where)
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        _check_object(data, schema, root, where)
    if isinstance(data, list):
        _check_array(data, schema, root, where)
    _check_choice(data, schema, root, where)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ loading
def folder(symbol: str) -> Path:
    """The contract folder of `symbol` (`core.WorldSpec`)."""
    path = HERE / symbol
    if not path.is_dir():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"no contract folder for {symbol}")
    return path


def python_object(dotted: str) -> Any:
    """`rllab.core.WorldSpec` to the object it names."""
    module, _, name = dotted.rpartition(".")
    return getattr(importlib.import_module(module), name)


def schema(symbol: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return json.loads((folder(symbol) / "schema.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def cases(symbol: str, kind: str = "samples") -> list[str]:
    """The case names of `symbol`'s samples (or recordings)."""
    return sorted(p.stem for p in (folder(symbol) / kind).glob("*.json"))


def raw(symbol: str, case: str, kind: str = "samples") -> Any:
    """A sample's (or recording's) JSON as stored."""
    path = folder(symbol) / kind / f"{case}.json"
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def sample(symbol: str, case: str) -> Any:
    """A type's sample, validated against its schema and decoded into the contract type."""
    data, spec = raw(symbol, case), schema(symbol)
    validate(data, spec)
    return decode(python_object(spec["x-python"]), data, f"{symbol}/{case}")


class Fixtures:
    """What a `double.py` or `fake.py` gets: its folder's files and the other contracts."""

    def __init__(self, symbol: str) -> None:
        self.symbol = symbol
        self.folder = folder(symbol)

    def recording(self, case: str) -> Any:
        return raw(self.symbol, case, "recordings")

    def decode(self, tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
        return decode(tp, data, self.symbol)

    def encode(self, value: Any) -> Any:
        return encode(value)

    def sample(self, symbol: str, case: str) -> Any:
        return sample(symbol, case)

    def double(self, symbol: str, case: str | None = None) -> Any:
        return double(symbol, case)


def _build(symbol: str, module: str, case: str | None, kind: str) -> Any:
    path = folder(symbol) / f"{module}.py"
    namespace = {"__name__": f"contract_{module}_{symbol.replace('.', '_')}"}
    exec(compile(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), str(path), "exec"), namespace)  # noqa: S102
    names = cases(symbol, kind)
    if case is None:
        if len(names) > 1:
            raise ValueError(f"{symbol} has several cases, name one of {names}")
        case = names[0] if names else ""
    return namespace["build"](Fixtures(symbol), case)


def double(symbol: str, case: str | None = None) -> Any:
    """A replay double serving `recordings/<case>.json` (the only one when `case` is None)."""
    return _build(symbol, "double", case, "recordings")


def fake(symbol: str, case: str | None = None) -> Any:
    """A fixture-backed fake of a boundary function, with the function's signature."""
    return _build(symbol, "fake", case, "recordings")

41	def encode(value: Any) -> Any:
42	    """The JSON form of a contract value."""
43	    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
44	        out = {f.name: encode(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
45	        kind = next((k for k, cls in KINDS.items() if isinstance(value, cls)), None)
46	        return {"kind": kind, **out} if kind else out
47	    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
48	        return {"dtype": str(value.dtype), "shape": list(value.shape), "data": value.tolist()}
49	    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
50	        return {str(k): encode(v) for k, v in value.items()}
51	    if isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
52	        return [encode(v) for v in value]
53	    if isinstance(value, Path):
54	        return str(value)
55	    if isinstance(value, dt.datetime):
56	        return value.isoformat()
57	    if isinstance(value, dt.timedelta):
58	        return value.total_seconds()
59	    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
60	        return value.item()
61	    return value

64	def _fail(where: str, why: str) -> typing.NoReturn:
65	    raise ValueError(f"{where or '<root>'}: {why}")

68	def _decode_dataclass(tp: type, data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
69	    if not isinstance(data, dict):
70	        _fail(where, f"expected an object for {tp.__name__}")
71	    data = dict(data)
72	    kind = data.pop("kind", None)
73	    if kind is not None and KINDS.get(kind) is not tp:
74	        _fail(where, f"kind {kind!r} is not {tp.__name__}")
75	    hints = typing.get_type_hints(tp)
76	    fields = {f.name: f for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)}
77	    if extra := set(data) - set(fields):
78	        _fail(where, f"unknown fields {sorted(extra)} for {tp.__name__}")
79	    missing = [
80	        n
81	        for n, f in fields.items()
82	        if n not in data
83	        and f.default is dataclasses.MISSING
84	        and f.default_factory is dataclasses.MISSING
85	    ]
86	    if missing:
87	        _fail(where, f"missing fields {missing} for {tp.__name__}")
88	    return tp(**{n: decode(hints[n], v, f"{where}.{n}") for n, v in data.items()})

91	def _decode_union(args: tuple[Any, ...], data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
92	    if data is None and type(None) in args:
93	        return None
94	    if isinstance(data, dict) and "kind" in data:
95	        cls = KINDS.get(data["kind"])
96	        if cls in args:
97	            return _decode_dataclass(cls, data, where)
98	    errors = []
99	    for arg in args:
100	        if arg is type(None):
101	            continue
102	        try:
103	            return decode(arg, data, where)
104	        except ValueError as exc:
105	            errors.append(str(exc))
106	    _fail(where, f"matches no member of the union: {errors}")

109	def _decode_array(data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
110	    if not isinstance(data, dict) or set(data) != {"dtype", "shape", "data"}:
111	        _fail(where, "expected an array object {dtype, shape, data}")
112	    array = np.asarray(data["data"], dtype=np.dtype(data["dtype"]))
113	    return array.reshape(data["shape"])

116	def _decode_scalar(tp: type, data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
117	    if tp is bool:
118	        if not isinstance(data, bool):
119	            _fail(where, f"expected a bool, got {data!r}")
120	        return data
121	    if tp is int:
122	        if not isinstance(data, int) or isinstance(data, bool):
123	            _fail(where, f"expected an int, got {data!r}")
124	        return data
125	    if tp is float:
126	        if not isinstance(data, (int, float)) or isinstance(data, bool):
127	            _fail(where, f"expected a number, got {data!r}")
128	        return float(data)
129	    if tp is str:
130	        if not isinstance(data, str):
131	            _fail(where, f"expected a string, got {data!r}")
132	        return data
133	    if tp is Path:
134	        return Path(decode(str, data, where))
135	    if tp is dt.datetime:
136	        return dt.datetime.fromisoformat(decode(str, data, where))
137	    if tp is dt.timedelta:
138	        return dt.timedelta(seconds=decode(float, data, where))
139	    _fail(where, f"no JSON form for {tp!r}")
140

142	def _decode_container(origin: Any, args: tuple[Any, ...], data: Any, where: str) -> Any:
143	    if origin in (Mapping, dict):
144	        if not isinstance(data, dict):
145	            _fail(where, "expected an object")
146	        key, value = args or (str, Any)
147	        return {decode(key, k, where): decode(value, v, f"{where}.{k}") for k, v in data.items()}
148	    if not isinstance(data, list):
149	        _fail(where, "expected a list")
150	    if origin is list:
151	        return [decode(args[0], v, f"{where}[{i}]") for i, v in enumerate(data)]
152	    if len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis:
153	        return tuple(decode(args[0], v, f"{where}[{i}]") for i, v in enumerate(data))
154	    if len(args) != len(data):
155	        _fail(where, f"expected {len(args)} items, got {len(data)}")
156	    return tuple(decode(a, v, f"{where}[{i}]") for i, (a, v) in enumerate(zip(args, data)))
157	
158	
159	def decode(tp: Any, data: Any, where: str = "") -> Any:
160	    """`data` (a JSON form) as a value of type `tp`, checked strictly; `ValueError` if not."""
161	    origin, args = typing.get_origin(tp), typing.get_args(tp)
162	    if tp is Any or tp is object:
163	        return data
164	    if isinstance(tp, typing.TypeAliasType):
165	        return decode(tp.__value__, data, where)
166	    if origin is typing.Literal:
167	        if data not in args:
168	            _fail(where, f"{data!r} is not one of {args}")
169	        return data
170	    if origin in (typing.Union, types.UnionType):
171	        return _decode_union(args, data, where)
172	    if origin is np.ndarray or tp is np.ndarray:
173	        return _decode_array(data, where)
174	    if origin in (Mapping, dict, list, tuple):
175	        return _decode_container(origin, args, data, where)
