wrote /Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r1/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-adb398ddea3959632/tests/contracts/test_doubles.py
"""Each replay double serves its recording, as the Protocol it stands for allows."""

import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.core import ActionError, Episode, Step
from rllab.methods import Transition
from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).parent / "contracts.py"))
RNG = np.random.default_rng(0)


def play(world, policy):
    """The recorded episode, stepped with the policy's actions."""
    step = world.reset(42)
    steps = [step]
    while not step.done:
        step = world.step(policy.act(step.obs, RNG))
        steps.append(step)
    return steps


def test_world_double_replays_its_seed_42_episode_with_the_rule_policy():
    world = C["double"]("core.World")
    steps = play(world, C["double"]("core.Policy"))
    assert world.spec.world == "snake"
    assert len(steps) == 6
    assert steps[-1].truncated
    assert world.spec.max_steps == 5
    assert [int(a[0]) for a in world.actions] == [2, 2, 2, 0, 2]
    assert sum(float(s.rewards.sum()) for s in steps) == world.result().scores["snake"] == 1.0
    assert world.seeds == [42]
    assert all(isinstance(s, Step) for s in steps)


def test_world_double_draws_the_board_of_each_step():
    world = C["double"]("core.World")
    world.reset(42)
    first = world.draw()
    world.step(np.array([2]))
    second = world.draw()
    assert (first.width, first.height) == (10.0, 10.0)
    head = [o for o in second.objects if o.kind == "head"]
    assert [(o.x, o.y, o.agent) for o in head] == [(3.0, 5.0, 0)]


def test_world_double_refuses_bad_actions_and_steps_after_done():
    world = C["double"]("core.World")
    world.reset(42)
    with pytest.raises(ActionError) as caught:
        world.step(np.array([3]))
    assert caught.value.agent == "snake"
    assert caught.value.value == 3
    for _ in range(5):
        world.step(np.array([2]))
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="ended"):
        world.step(np.array([2]))


def test_policy_double_has_no_action_for_an_unrecorded_observation():
    policy = C["double"]("core.Policy")
    with pytest.raises(LookupError):
        policy.act(np.zeros((1, 12), np.float32), RNG)


def test_play_episode_double_serves_the_traced_seed_7_episode():
    play_episode = C["double"]("core.play_episode")
    world = C["double"]("core.World")
    episode = play_episode(world, {"snake": C["double"]("core.Policy")}, 7, trace=True)
    assert isinstance(episode, Episode)
    assert episode.result.seed == 7
    assert len(episode.frames) == episode.result.length + 1
    untraced = play_episode(world, {"snake": C["double"]("core.Policy")}, 7)
    assert untraced.frames is None
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="snake"):
        play_episode(world, {}, 7)


def test_check_world_doubles_serve_states_and_best_actions():
    corridor = C["double"]("worlds.CheckWorld", "two_exit_corridor")
    assert corridor.states().shape == (6, corridor.spec.obs_len)
    assert corridor.best_actions().tolist() == [0, 0, 1, 1, 1, 1]
    point = C["double"]("worlds.CheckWorld", "target_point")
    assert point.states().shape == (6, 1)
    assert np.sign(point.states()[:, 0]).tolist() == (-point.best_actions()[:, 0]).tolist()
    start = point.reset(0)
    assert start.obs.shape == (1, 1)
    with pytest.raises(ActionError):
        point.step(np.array([[1.5]], np.float32))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["snake_rule_5_obs", "fish_rule_no_ball"])
def test_rule_policy_doubles_have_a_one_sentence_description(case):
    rule = C["double"]("baselines.RulePolicy", case)
    assert rule.description.endswith(".")
    assert rule.description.count(". ") == 0


def test_fish_rule_double_pushes_inside_the_box_when_it_sees_no_ball():
    rule = C["double"]("baselines.RulePolicy", "fish_rule_no_ball")
    obs = C["raw"]("baselines.RulePolicy", "fish_rule_no_ball", "recordings")["calls"][0]["obs"]
    push = rule.act(C["decode"](np.ndarray, obs), RNG)
    assert push.shape == (1, 2)
    assert np.all(np.abs(push) <= 1.0)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", ["snake_5_actions", "fish_5_pushes"])
def test_random_policy_doubles_serve_five_actions_inside_their_space(case):
    policy = C["double"]("baselines.RandomPolicy", case)
    actions = [policy.act(policy.obs, RNG) for _ in range(5)]
    space = policy.action
    for a in actions:
        if hasattr(space, "n"):
            assert a.shape == (1,)
            assert 0 <= int(a[0]) < space.n
        else:
            assert a.shape == (1, space.dims)
            assert np.all((a >= space.low) & (a <= space.high))
    with pytest.raises(LookupError):
        policy.act(policy.obs, RNG)


def test_learner_double_acts_observes_and_freezes_a_policy():
    learner = C["double"]("methods.Learner")
    world = C["double"]("core.World")
    step = world.reset(1)
    action = learner.act(step.obs, RNG)
    nxt = world.step(action)
    t = Transition(step.obs, action, nxt.rewards, nxt.obs, nxt.done, nxt.truncated, nxt.active)
    learner.observe(t)
    assert learner.observed == [t]
    assert len(learner.recorded) == 10
    assert int(learner.policy().act(step.obs, RNG)[0]) == 2
    state = learner.state_dict()
    state["steps"] = -1
    assert learner.state_dict()["steps"] == 10


def test_run_double_serves_a_finished_three_checkpoint_run():
    run = C["double"]("runlog.Run")
    assert run.record.status == "finished"
    assert [c.pct for c in run.checkpoints()] == [0, 50, 100]
    evals = run.evals()
    assert len(evals) == 21
    assert evals[-1].step == run.record.config.budget
    assert all(e.scores.keys() == {"snake"} for e in evals)
    assert len(run.episodes()) == 8


def test_run_writer_double_keeps_the_writes_and_closes_with_the_status(tmp_path):
    writer_class = C["double"]("runlog.RunWriter")
    config = C["sample"]("train.RunConfig", "snake_default")
    spec = C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default")
    writer = writer_class(tmp_path, config, spec)
    writer.episode(C["sample"]("core.EpisodeResult", "snake_12_food"))
    writer.eval(C["sample"]("runlog.EvalPoint", "snake_50pct"))
    ckpt = writer.checkpoint(0, 0, {"snake": {"steps": 0}})
    record = writer.close("finished")
    assert ckpt.path == tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / config.name / "checkpoints" / "ckpt_0.pt"
    assert ckpt.methods == dict(config.methods)
    assert (len(writer.episodes), len(writer.evals), len(writer.checkpoints)) == (1, 1, 1)
    assert record.status == "finished"
    assert record.config == config
    assert record.started == writer_class.recorded.started
    (tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / config.name).mkdir(parents=True)
    with pytest.raises(RunExistsError):
        writer_class(tmp_path, config, spec)

"""Each boundary function's fake serves its fixtures and raises the contract's errors."""

import inspect
import json
import runpy
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest
import torch

import rllab.baselines
import rllab.charts
import rllab.evaluate
import rllab.methods
import rllab.runlog
import rllab.train
import rllab.worlds
from rllab.core import Box, Output
from rllab.methods import SpecMismatchError
from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError

C = runpy.run_path(str(Path(__file__).parent / "contracts.py"))
RNG = np.random.default_rng(0)

FAKES = {
    "worlds.make": rllab.worlds.make,
    "baselines.rule_for": rllab.baselines.rule_for,
    "baselines.baseline_means": rllab.baselines.baseline_means,
    "methods.make_learner": rllab.methods.make_learner,
    "methods.load_policy": rllab.methods.load_policy,
    "train.load_run_configs": rllab.train.load_run_configs,
    "train.run_check": rllab.train.run_check,
    "train.run_train": rllab.train.run_train,
    "train.run_batch": rllab.train.run_batch,
    "runlog.find_runs": rllab.runlog.find_runs,
    "runlog.open_run": rllab.runlog.open_run,
    "runlog.progress": rllab.runlog.progress,
    "charts.learning_curve": rllab.charts.learning_curve,
    "evaluate.final_evaluation": rllab.evaluate.final_evaluation,
}


def params(fn):
    return [(p.name, p.kind, p.default) for p in inspect.signature(fn).parameters.values()]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", sorted(FAKES))
def test_fake_has_the_contract_signature(symbol):
    assert params(C["fake"](symbol)) == params(FAKES[symbol])


def test_make_fake_gives_the_world_double_and_refuses_other_worlds():
    make = C["fake"]("worlds.make")
    world = make("snake")
    assert world.spec.world == "snake"
    assert world.reset(42).obs.shape == (1, world.spec.obs_len)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="fish"):
        make("fish", "one")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="width"):
        make("snake", width=12)


def test_rule_for_fake_gives_the_snake_rule_and_a_key_error_otherwise():
    rule_for = C["fake"]("baselines.rule_for")
    rule = rule_for("snake", "default", "snake")
    assert "shorter axis" in rule.description
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="fish"):
        rule_for("fish", "one", "fish")


def test_baseline_means_fake_serves_the_snake_means():
    means = C["fake"]("baselines.baseline_means")("snake", "default")
    assert means == C["sample"]("baselines.BaselineMeans", "snake")
    assert means.rule["snake"] > means.random["snake"]
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        C["fake"]("baselines.baseline_means")("prey", "default")


def test_make_learner_fake_builds_dqn_for_snake_and_rejects_a_box():
    make_learner = C["fake"]("methods.make_learner")
    config = C["sample"]("methods.MethodConfig", "snake_dqn_defaults")
    spec = C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default")
    learner = make_learner(config, spec, "snake", RNG)
    assert len(learner.state_dict()) == 5
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="dqn needs a Discrete action space, not Box"):
        make_learner(config, C["sample"]("core.WorldSpec", "fish_local"), "fish", RNG)


def test_load_policy_fake_gives_the_rule_and_names_differing_spec_fields():
    load_policy = C["fake"]("methods.load_policy")
    ckpt = C["sample"]("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake_100pct")
    policy = load_policy(ckpt, "snake", ckpt.spec)
    first = C["double"]("core.World").reset(42).obs
    assert int(policy.act(first, RNG)[0]) == 2
    changed = type(ckpt.spec)(**{**vars(ckpt.spec), "obs_len": 14, "action": Box(2, -1.0, 1.0)})
    with pytest.raises(SpecMismatchError) as caught:
        load_policy(ckpt, "snake", changed)
    assert caught.value.fields == ("obs_len", "action")


def test_load_run_configs_fake_names_each_config_by_stem_and_seed():
    configs = C["fake"]("train.load_run_configs")(Path("configs/snake-default.toml"), [1, 2])
    assert [c.name for c in configs] == ["snake-default-s1", "snake-default-s2"]
    assert [c.seed for c in configs] == [1, 2]
    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
        C["fake"]("train.load_run_configs")(Path("configs/fish-one.toml"), [1])

    assert results == [C["sample"]("train.CheckResult", "dqn_pass")]


def test_run_train_fake_writes_the_finished_run_folder(tmp_path):
    run_train = C["fake"]("train.run_train")
    config = C["sample"]("train.RunConfig", "snake_default")
    record = run_train(config, tmp_path)
    folder = tmp_path / "snake" / "default" / config.name
    assert record.path == folder
    assert record.status == "finished"
    assert json.loads((folder / "run.json").read_text())["status"] == "finished"
    evals = (folder / "evals.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()
    assert len(evals) == 21
    ckpts = sorted(p.name for p in (folder / "checkpoints").iterdir())
    assert ckpts == ["ckpt_0.pt", "ckpt_100.pt", "ckpt_50.pt"]
    saved = torch.load(folder / "checkpoints" / "ckpt_100.pt", weights_only=True)
    assert (saved["pct"], saved["run"]) == (100, config.name)
    with pytest.raises(RunExistsError):
        run_train(config, tmp_path)

    assert find_runs(tmp_path, "fish", "one") == []

"""The M1 interface as code: exports, step and entry signatures, and the `rllab` dispatch."""

import importlib
import inspect
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.cli import commands, main

EXPORTS = {
    "core": {
        "EVAL_SEEDS", "FINAL_SEEDS", "ActionError", "ActionSpace", "Array", "Box", "Direction",
        "Discrete", "DrawState", "Drawable", "Episode", "EpisodeResult", "Frame", "MeasureSpec",
        "Output", "Policy", "Settings", "Step", "TeamSpec", "World", "WorldSpec", "play_episode",
    },
    "worlds": {"CheckWorld", "make"},
    "baselines": {"BaselineMeans", "RandomPolicy", "RulePolicy", "baseline_means", "rule_for"},
    "methods": {
        "Learner", "MethodConfig", "NonFiniteError", "SpecMismatchError", "Transition",
        "load_policy", "make_learner",
    },
    "train": {
        "CheckFailedError", "CheckResult", "RunConfig", "load_run_configs", "run_batch",
        "run_check", "run_train",
    },
    "runlog": {
        "Checkpoint", "EvalPoint", "Failure", "Progress", "Run", "RunExistsError", "RunRecord",
        "RunWriter", "find_runs", "open_run", "progress",
    },
    "charts": {"MixedRunsError", "learning_curve"},
    "evaluate": {"Comparison", "Summary", "Verdict", "compare", "final_evaluation"},
    "cli": {"main"},
}  # fmt: skip

# Each step and entry stub: module, name, parameter names, the task that replaces it.

    ("core.episode", "play_episode", "world policies seed trace", "M1-T18"),
    ("worlds.make", "make", "world variant overrides", "M1-T05"),

    ("cli.pipelines.pl05_chart", "run_chart", "root world variant out include", "M1-T23"),
    ("cli.pipelines.pl06_evaluate", "run_evaluate", "root world variant out include", "M1-T24"),
]  # fmt: skip

# Step classes: module, name, constructor parameters, the task that replaces them.
CLASSES = [
    ("worlds.snake", "Snake", "overrides", "M1-T06"),
    ("worlds.checks", "CheckDiscrete", "overrides", "M1-T04"),
    ("baselines.random", "RandomPolicy", "action", "M1-T14"),
    ("baselines.snake", "SnakeRule", "", "M1-T15"),
    ("methods.dqn.learner", "DQN", "config spec team rng", "M1-T08"),
    ("runlog.writer", "RunWriter", "root config spec", "M1-T09"),
]


def stub(module, name):
    return getattr(importlib.import_module(f"rllab.{module}"), name)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("component", sorted(EXPORTS))
def test_component_exports_its_contract(component):
    module = importlib.import_module(f"rllab.{component}")
    assert set(module.__all__) == EXPORTS[component]
    assert all(hasattr(module, name) for name in module.__all__)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("module", "name", "names", "task"), STUBS)
def test_step_stub_has_its_signature_and_names_its_task(module, name, names, task):
    fn = stub(module, name)
    assert list(inspect.signature(fn).parameters) == names.split()
    args = [None] * sum(
        p.default is p.empty and p.kind is p.POSITIONAL_OR_KEYWORD
        for p in inspect.signature(fn).parameters.values()
    )
    kwargs = {"rng": None} if name == "compare" else {}
    with pytest.raises(NotImplementedError, match=task):
        fn(*args, **kwargs)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(("module", "name", "names", "task"), CLASSES)
def test_step_class_has_its_constructor_and_names_its_task(module, name, names, task):
    cls = stub(module, name)
    params = list(inspect.signature(cls.__init__).parameters)[1:]
    assert params == names.split()
    args = [None] * sum(n != "overrides" for n in params)
    with pytest.raises(NotImplementedError, match=task):
        cls(*args)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("module", "task"),
    [
        ("train.pipelines.pl01_check", "M1-T19"),
        ("train.pipelines.pl02_train", "M1-T20"),
        ("train.pipelines.pl03_batch", "M1-T21"),
        ("cli.pipelines.pl04_watch", "M1-T22"),
        ("cli.pipelines.pl05_chart", "M1-T23"),
        ("cli.pipelines.pl06_evaluate", "M1-T24"),
    ],
)
def test_each_entry_module_has_its_smoke_run(module, task):
    with pytest.raises(NotImplementedError, match=task):
        stub(module, "smoke")()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("argv", "task"),
    [
        (["check", "configs/snake-default.toml"], "M1-T19"),
        (["check", "--smoke"], "M1-T19"),
        (["train", "configs/snake-default.toml", "--seed", "3"], "M1-T21"),
        (["train", "--smoke"], "M1-T20"),
        (["batch", "configs/snake-default.toml", "--seeds", "1-5"], "M1-T21"),
        (["watch", "runs/snake/default/snake-default-s1"], "M1-T22"),
        (["chart", "snake", "default", "--include", "snake-default-s6"], "M1-T23"),
        (["evaluate", "snake"], "M1-T24"),
    ],
)
def test_main_dispatches_each_command_and_prints_one_error_line(argv, task, capsys):
    assert main(argv) == 1
    err = capsys.readouterr().err.strip().splitlines()
    assert len(err) == 1
    assert err[0].startswith(f"rllab {argv[0]}: NotImplementedError: {task}")


def test_main_names_a_missing_argument_in_one_line(capsys):
    assert main(["watch"]) == 1
    assert capsys.readouterr().err.strip() == "rllab watch: ValueError: a run path is needed (or --smoke)"


def test_main_prints_each_returned_item(monkeypatch, capsys):
    monkeypatch.setattr(commands.pl04_watch, "run_watch", lambda path: f"progress of {path}")
    assert main(["watch", "runs/x"]) == 0
    assert capsys.readouterr().out == f"progress of {Path('runs/x')}\n"


def test_parse_seeds_reads_ranges_and_lists():
    assert commands.parse_seeds("1-5") == [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
    assert commands.parse_seeds("1,3,7-8") == [1, 3, 7, 8]


def test_errors_name_their_cause():
    from rllab.charts import MixedRunsError
    from rllab.core import ActionError
    from rllab.methods import NonFiniteError, SpecMismatchError
    from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError
    from rllab.train import CheckFailedError, CheckResult

    assert isinstance(ActionError("snake", 3), ValueError)
    assert "snake" in str(ActionError("snake", 3))
    assert NonFiniteError(float("nan")).value != NonFiniteError(float("nan")).value
    assert str(SpecMismatchError(["obs_len"])).endswith("obs_len")
    assert "lr" in str(MixedRunsError(["lr", "budget"]))
    assert isinstance(RunExistsError(Path("runs/x")), FileExistsError)
    failed = CheckFailedError(CheckResult("ppo", "check-continuous", 0.8, False))
    assert "0.80" in str(failed)
    assert failed.result.method == "ppo"
    assert np.isnan(NonFiniteError(float("nan")).value)
